					level = 6
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}

						}
					}
				}
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					limit = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = air_base
							size > 5
						}
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						type = air_base
						level = 6
						instant_build = yes
					}
					set_state_flag = GER_expanding_the_luftwaffe_flag
				}				
			}

			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					air_base > 3
					air_base < 9
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = air_base
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
				}
				set_state_flag = GER_expanding_the_luftwaffe_flag
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {

				limit = {
					capital_scope = { is_on_continent = europe }
					is_subject = no
					#not = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
					not = { has_government = fascism }

					not = { tag = ENG }
					not = { tag = SOV }

				}

		}
